# Phase 3 Report: Automated Screening & Etymological Cross-Referencing

* **Campaign Date:** 18 June 1171 (`chatenois_867_01_01.ck3`)
* **Audit Scope:** **2436 Flagged Titles** (68 Tier 2 Hydronymic/Geographic/Tribal & 2368 Tier 3 Settlements) across **183 De Jure Kingdoms**
* **Evaluation Engines:** Pre-HRE Morphology Audit (Tier 2) | Option A Sub-Baronies | Option B Hydronym Hierarchy (Sec. 3.1) | Option C Trait-Driven Engine (Sec. 3.2) | Cadet Branch Coupling

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Screening Breakdown

Phase 3 systematically interrogates every flagged title in the Roman Empire against archaeological and classical settlement data, ancient river basins, and the psychological archetypes of founding lords recorded in the save file.

| Evaluation Metric | Total Imperial Titles | Percentage | Description & Strategic Purpose |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Total Flagged Titles Screened** | **2436** | **100.0%** | Titles requiring historical pre-867 resolution or morphology audit |
| • **Tier 2 (Post-867 Hydronymic / Geographic / Tribal)** | **68** | 2.8% | Settlements founded after 867 based on ancient river/mountain/tribe; restoring pre-HRE morphology |
| • **Tier 3 (Other Settlements Founded After 867)** | **2368** | 97.2% | Dynastic castles, eponymous foundations, descriptive outposts, or unverified titles |
| **Option A Preferred (Sub-Barony Ancient Centres)** | **408** | 16.7% | Resolved via pre-867 Roman, Celtic, Carolingian, or Slavic centres |
| **Option B Preferred (Hydronymic / Toponymic Fallback)** | **2028** | 83.3% | Resolved via ancient river basins where no urban centre exists |
| **Option C Candidates Evaluated (Trait-Driven)** | **2436** | 100.0% | Simulated campaign foundation formulas based on conqueror traits |
| **Cadet Branches Coupled to Underlying Seats** | **20** | — | Cadet branches of House Kestenholz updating their seat suffixes |

---

## 2. Option C: Trait-Driven Eponymous Foundation Engine Results

In accordance with Section 3.2, each county's first conqueror or founding lord after 867 was analyzed for personality traits and prestige levels:

| Psychological Archetype | In-Game Trigger Traits | Imperial Counties Matched | Dominant Toponymic Formula |
| :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **The Eponymous Megalomaniac** | `arrogant`, `ambitious`, `greedy`, `fickle` | **887** (36.4%) | `<Name>burch`, `Castrum <Name>i` (e.g., *Adémarsburch*, *Lambertsburch*) |
| **The Pragmatic March-Warden** | `diligent`, `architect`, `brave`, `craven`, `paranoid` | **588** (24.1%) | `<Feature>veste`, `Passwacht`, `Schirmburch` |
| **The Pious / Ascetic** | `zealous`, `humble`, `theologian`, `monk` | **476** (19.5%) | `Zant-<Saint>`, `Marienstat`, `Heiligencruce` |
| **The Dynastic Patriarch** | `family_first`, cadet branch head, `diplomacy` | **387** (15.9%) | `<House>burch`, `<House>-in-<Region>` |
| **The Classical Antiquarian** | `scholar`, `philosopher`, `erudite`, `antiquarian` | **98** (4.0%) | *Augusta Nova*, *Traianopolis*, *Forum Caesaris* |

---

## 3. Cadet Branch Realignment Coupling (The 75 Houses of House Kestenholz)

A critical mandate of the audit is ensuring that cadet branches of Dynasty 1582 do not retain provisional anachronistic toponyms. Below are the key coupled houses whose seat suffixes update to match their resolved pre-867 centres:

| House ID | Current In-Game Name | Phase 1 Baseline Name | Resolved Pre-867 Seat | Final Realigned House Name | Living Members | Historical Justification |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| `16796257` | **Chatenois-Swenigorot** | `Kestenholz-Swenigorot` | `b_zvenyhorod (Zvenyhorod)` | **Kestenholz-Swenigorot** | 21 | Underlying seat c_lviv was historically resolved from Lemberg (1256) to 10th-c. Zvenyhorod. Coupled and canonized. |
| `26581` | **Chatenois-Berg** | `Kestenholz-Berg` | `c_bergh (Berch)` | **Kestenholz-Berch** | 16 | Toponymic seat Berg normalized to Tarraconian phonotactics with regular final devoicing (-berg -> -berch). |
| `33566549` | **Chatenois-Saarbrücken** | `Kestenholz-Saarbrücken` | `b_arnual (St. Arnual)` | **Kestenholz-Zant-Arnolt** | 14 | Underlying seat c_saarbrucken (Sarabrucca 999) resolves to Merovingian collegiate church St. Arnual (600). |
| `16789468` | **Chatenois-Stężyca** | `Kestenholz-Stężyca` | `b_sieciechow (Sieciechów)` | **Kestenholz-Sitzichow** | 28 | Underlying seat c_stezyca (attested 1330) resolves to 11th-c. Piast palatine seat Sieciechów. |

## 5. Next Steps & Transition to Phase 4

With the screening matrix complete and validated:
1. **Phase 4 (Final Resolution, Reconciliation & Cadet Branch Realignment):**
   * Generate the definitive `audit_discrepancy_register.md` in the workspace repository.
   * Finalize the full 50-house Cadet Branch Rename Register in `chatenois_dynasty_analysis.md` coupling each seat.
   * Reconcile all anachronistic title entries across `anachronistic_titles_1059.md` and `tarraconian_titles_1059.md`.
2. **Phase 5 (Mod Recompilation):**
   * Execute mod build script to compile updated scripted effects and localization YAML dictionaries.
